6. Foreclosure Chain of Authority in 17CV38591: July 30, 2019 execution date vs August 7, 2019 docketing date (2:27 PM) vs July 31, 2019 citation across Writs; Turning Leaf Homes LLC 12-person jury trial canceled ex parte on August 7, 2019 upon entry of void default judgment; Clackamas County Sheriff pre-sale certified mailings (Sheriff Case 23-20024) completely omitting recorded fee owner Annika Eriksson with 5 returned envelopes; Amber L. Labrecque written credit bid ($465,396.39) on behalf of foreclosing trust beneficiary CWALT 2006-OC10; Rain City Capital of Oregon noted as third-party attendee/bidder, with Certificate of Sale and Sheriff's Deed issued to BNYM CWALT 2006-OC10; Post-objection hearing sequence and Judge Wetzel April 2/8, 2024 order.
7. Klinedinst PC Investigation in 24CV21417: Gregor A. Hensrude & Chancellor K. Eagle drafted June 3, 2026 proposed judgment attempting nunc pro tunc reformation; received Annika's June 10 email objection; served June 10 revised proposed judgment conceding July 30 signing and August 7 entry; and filed June 22 response brief with binding judicial admission of July 26 case closure and July 30 signing.
8. Julius AI Scientific & Governance Integration: Litigation readiness review of microclimate/LST data; comparator analysis of Burgard, Kelvin & Dunlap, and Nicita cases against Oregon City.

MEDIUM:        Email (Email)
ACTORS:        From: Annika Eriksson (`erikssona@icloud.com`) -> To: Josh Wheeler, PE (Assistant City Engineer, `jwheeler@orcity.org`) [Public Works & Utilities]

FACTUAL & FORENSIC SUMMARY:
Eriksson notes she owns 12054 Chapin Court (acquired via Bargain & Sale Deed recorded Dec 21, 2021) and previously worked for Turning Leaf Homes, LLC. She explains that during Turning Leaf Homes' ownership, the City recorded two substantial municipal code enforcement liens ($72,026.00 and $14,601.00), part of which covered sidewalk repair and tree removal. She inquires whether the City's Sidewalk Reimbursement Assistance Program could be applied retroactively to reduce the lien balance.

RECORD 007 OF 107 | ITEM 1.6 | 1. PHASE I: PRE-DISPUTE INQUIRIES & ACCOUNT ESTABLISHMENT (JUNE 2023 – MAY 2024)
================================================================================
TRACK / LAYER: Forensic Record
DATE & TIME:   January 11–24, 2024
TITLE:         Unlawful $867.62 Prior-Tenant Door Hanger, Physical Shutoff & Project Access NOW Payment ($311.02)
MEDIUM:        Door Hanger Notice / Physical Disconnection / Charitable Electronic Payment (Notice / Letter)
ACTORS:        From: Jonathan (Project Access NOW) on behalf of Annika Eriksson (Account `01-466348-05`) -> To: Oregon City Utility Billing (Ashley Flues, John Oreskovich, Layla Cnossen) [Public Works & Utilities]
SUBJECT:       

FACTUAL & FORENSIC SUMMARY:
On January 11, 2024, City Utility Billing staffer Ashley Flues logs an internal note combining prior tenant Stephanie Jones's unpaid $606.60 balance (`01-466348-04`) with Eriksson's first two water bills ($116.79 + $119.23 = $236.02 on Account `01-466348-05`) plus a $25.00 door hanger fee to post a door hanger demanding **$867.62** (`$606.60 + $236.02 + $25.00 = $867.62`). On January 22, 2024, the City physically shuts off water service and adds a $50.00 shutoff fee, bringing Eriksson's Account `01-466348-05` balance to **$311.02** (`$236.02 + $25.00 + $50.00`). On January 23, 2024 (credited January 24, 2024, Batch `B00015794`, Receipt `R00738478`), **Jonathan from Project Access NOW** pays **$311.02** via VISA card (`*4447`, Auth `613634`) on Eriksson's behalf to restore water service. That same day, John Oreskovich assesses an additional $50.00 meter tampering fee (`UBPKT06405`, bringing January fees to $125.00 total).

RECORD 010 OF 107 | ITEM 1.9A | 1. PHASE I: PRE-DISPUTE INQUIRIES & ACCOUNT ESTABLISHMENT (JUNE 2023 – MAY 2024)
================================================================================
TRACK / LAYER: Forensic Record
DATE & TIME:   February 27, 2024 (Morning)
TITLE:         Municipal Traffic Hearing, Ex Parte Officer Whispering, Parking Lot Interception & Docket No. 26301707
MEDIUM:        In-Person Municipal Court Proceeding / Parking Lot Enforcement Action (Court Filing / Order)
ACTORS:        From:  -> To:  [Other / General]
SUBJECT:       

FACTUAL & FORENSIC SUMMARY:
Preceded by pretextual traffic stops of Eriksson's turquoise 1995 Mazda B2300 work pickup truck (first late at night in the Winco parking lot citing visible body damage from a declared insurance total loss, and second days later en route to the library citing expired registration/insurance and address update delays), Judge Lindgren had granted an initial 2- to 3-week grace period to bring documentation into compliance. On February 27, 2024, Donald Buckhout drives Eriksson to Municipal Court for her follow-up appearance. Mr. Buckhout's driver's license was suspended due to unresolved administrative fines resulting from severe mail delivery disruptions during prior displacement. While Mr. Buckhout waits inside the truck in the municipal court parking lot, a police officer observes and identifies the vehicle. The officer enters the courtroom during Eriksson's hearing and whispers ex parte directly to Judge Lindgren on the bench. Immediately following this off-the-record communication, Judge Lindgren's demeanor shifts from leniency to hostility: looking out the courtroom window at the truck, she sternly confronts and interrogates Eriksson, accusing her of perjury: *"Ms. Eriksson, if I find out you lied to me..."* Although Eriksson truthfully confirms that Buckhout operated the vehicle, officers immediately intercept Buckhout in the parking lot and issue citations on the spot: issuing Oregon City Municipal Court Docket No. `26301707` to Donald Buckhout for driving while suspended, and issuing Citation No. `27300817` to Annika Eriksson for driving without insurance / permitting an unauthorized driver. The truck is temporarily held in the municipal court parking lot pending proof of insurance coverage.

SUBJECT:       `Re: *** Urgent! Your Garbage Account ***`

FACTUAL & FORENSIC SUMMARY:
* *March 27, 2024 at 2:15 PM (Eriksson):* Eriksson writes explaining extreme financial hardship: *"I am currently waiting on an unemployment claim and seeking work, but struggling to pay all of my bills this month. If you can offer to break up the balance on my account over the next few months I hope to be able to begin making payments in a few weeks."*
  * *March 27, 2024 at 2:25 PM (Wolter):* Wolter responds: *"Annika, we will try to work with you, but we've a problem in that we have never been paid since the service was started. At this time, there are two outstanding invoices: 1. Our invoice dated 12/27/23 - $39.37 – due 01/15/24; 2. Our invoice dated 02/28/24 - $66.21 – due upon receipt. Please let us know exactly what you will pay and when."*
  * *March 27, 2024 at 3:39 PM (Eriksson):* Proposes paying half of the first invoice the following week and the rest the week after, followed by a payment date for the second invoice.
  * *March 28, 2024 at 7:33 AM (Wolter):* Rejects partial plan: *"Payments 1 and 2 are not enough to keep the service going. I need to know when you'll pay the $66.21 invoice."*
  * *March 28, 2024 at 3:41 PM (Eriksson):* Discloses qualification for state-sponsored assistance: *"I've qualified for flex funds through the Oregon Health Plan to help with things like this. They will be able to pay that second invoice... Based on that, I can confidently say you will be paid: 1. $86.21 by 04/05/24; 2. $19.37 by 04/12/24. Would that work to keep service going? It's the best I can do right now but I have to admit I messed up and didn't get the containers out on the street last week and now we have mice, so please let me know if you can't accept the above terms."*
  * *March 28, 2024 at 3:46 PM (Wolter):* Agrees to arrangement: *"We'll go with your Number 1 and 2. Your numbers are different than mine – and much better! Together, they are way perfect. If you pay as promised, everything will be perfect. Thanks so much! Margo"*
  * *March 29, 2024 at 7:12:06 AM (Wolter):* Confirms receipt and agreement: *"Thanks, Annika! Margo"*

TRACK / LAYER: Forensic Record
DATE & TIME:   November 1, 2024
TITLE:         Building Official Sayers Written Sewer Gas Admission
MEDIUM:        Official Written Administrative Letter (Notice / Letter)
ACTORS:        From: James Sayers (Oregon City Building Official) -> To: Annika Eriksson / 12054 Chapin Court [Public Works & Utilities]
SUBJECT:       

FACTUAL & FORENSIC SUMMARY:
Sayers formally declares the residence a "Dangerous Building" under OCMC 15.24.010(15), admitting that the City's prolonged water shutoff created the severe unsanitary condition: *"Without running water, the sewer gases come up through the drainpipes and contaminate the living areas... home is considered a Dangerous Building."*

DATE & TIME:   November 2024 – March 2025
TITLE:         Shellpoint Curing Check ($2,863.77) Suppression
MEDIUM:        Administrative Processing & Real Estate Broker Communications (Other)
ACTORS:        From:  -> To:  [Other / General]
SUBJECT:       

FACTUAL & FORENSIC SUMMARY:
On November 5, 2024, loan servicer Shellpoint Mortgage Servicing issues an escrow curing check in the amount of $2,863.77 payable to the City for 12054 Chapin Court. Fraijo holds the check for four months without crediting the utility account or notifying Eriksson, while discussing property records with broker Damien Pogue. In March 2025, Fraijo returns the check to Shellpoint unapplied on the pretext that the City "could not determine what the check was for."

================================================================================
TRACK / LAYER: Forensic Record
DATE & TIME:   June 16, 2025
TITLE:         Code Enforcement Inspection Notice: Solid Waste & Overgrown Vegetation
MEDIUM:        Written Inspection Letter (Notice / Letter)
ACTORS:        From: Todd Kennedy (Oregon City Police Department Code Enforcement Division, 1234 Linn Ave, Oregon City, 503-905-3665) -> To: Annika Eriksson / 12054 Chapin Court [Code Enforcement]
SUBJECT:       

FACTUAL & FORENSIC SUMMARY:
Officer Kennedy reports conducting an on-site inspection of 12054 Chapin Court and finding code violations directly resulting from the City's prolonged water shutoff and disrupted sanitation: *"I have completed a site inspection and confirmed there is overgrown vegetation in the front, side and back yard. Further, I also observed that there appears to be overgrown weeds in the planting strip in front of the property as well as solid waste being stored in the side yard... Finally, please remove all solid waste being stored on the side yard of this property. This property will be evaluated for your voluntary compliance in 10 days (06/26/2025)."*

# Oregon City Public Voices and Oregon Homes Rulemaking
## Repository coverage, historical enforcement concerns, and an evidence-based assessment of Annika Eriksson's participation

Research date: October 13, 2026. This is targeted, preliminary public-records research—not an exhaustive audit, a finding of misconduct, or a legal opinion.

## 1. Findings in brief

**Other residents have raised concrete, related concerns over many years.** August 2013 minutes record residents challenging sidewalk/tree repair obligations, limited-income burdens, and deadlines. October 2015 minutes record calls for staff to reach the Commission without fear of retaliation and concerns about the City Attorney's relationship to the manager. November 2019 minutes document a neighbor dispute, a due-process concern, and questions about complaints against City staff. These are meaningful leads; the minutes establish that concerns were raised, not that the City violated the law. [C1–C3]

**Steve Varga remains unverified.** The recalled remark and his underlying situation were not located. Twenty recent recording-caption panels, three recently uploaded historical recordings, name variants, and relevant packet searches were checked. One additional historical-review recording could not be reliably opened. Automatic captions can miss names or wording; this search does not establish that he did not speak. No property, enforcement case, or motive has been assigned to him.

**Your written participation was formally recorded.** The published Phase One roster identifies Annika Eriksson as an individual bringing lived experience. Earlier submissions, the July 7 comment, and the September 28 submission updated September 30 are publicly preserved. The last of these is included in the October 22 Commission pack

ACTORS:        From: Jacob Burgard (`jacobburgard@gmail.com`) -> To: Alexandra Troutman (formerly Rains, Assistant City Manager, `atroutman@orcity.org`) [City Leadership]
SUBJECT:       `RE: Public Records Request / City ADA Title II Policy & Coordinator Designation`

FACTUAL & FORENSIC SUMMARY:
Following Alexandra Troutman's September 30, 2025 email claiming that Oregon City's ADA policy and interactive accommodation process applied solely to City employees, Jacob Burgard (plaintiff in *Burgard v. City of Oregon City*, `3:25-cv-01198-AR`) rebuts in writing: *"Under Title II of the Americans with Disabilities Act, 28 C.F.R. § 35.107, any public entity employing 50 or more persons is federally mandated to adopt and publish grievance procedures... This requirement applies to services, programs, and activities provided to members of the public, not merely internal employee accommodations. Please provide the contact information for Oregon City's designated ADA Coordinator for public services."* Burgard shares this communication directly with Annika Eriksson.

TRACK / LAYER: Forensic Record
DATE & TIME:   October 26, 2025 (5:38–5:39 PM)
TITLE:         Burgard & Eriksson Discovery and Webarchive Capture of Hastily Assembled Draft ADA Grievance Page
MEDIUM:        Webarchive Capture & Email Coordination (Email)
ACTORS:        From: Jacob Burgard & Annika Eriksson -> To: City of Oregon City Public Portal / Evidentiary Archive [Plaintiffs]
SUBJECT:       `Discovery of Live Unedited Draft ADA Grievance Page (OC_ADA Grievance | Oregon City, OR.webarchive)`

FACTUAL & FORENSIC SUMMARY:
Annika Eriksson discovers and alerts Jacob Burgard that Oregon City has hastily published an ADA Grievance page (`https://www.orcity.org/3458/Americans-with-Disabilities-Act-ADA`), attempting to fabricate retroactive Title II compliance following Burgard's records requests and Troutman's Sept 30 email. Burgard immediately inspects and archives the webpage (`OC_ADA Grievance | Oregon City, OR.webarchive`, 5.3 MB; text in `ADA grievance website archive.txt`), emailing Eriksson: *"Oh this is definitely new… It's being created now. The pages have filler content. The coordinator position is being filled in by Alex Troutman the assistant city manager. They have filler info for all that. This is 100% because of me."* The captured page contains literal unedited template placeholders: `Name: [ADA Coordinator Name]`, `Email: ada@oregoncity.gov`, `Phone: (XXX) XXX-XXXX`, `Address: [ADA Coordinator Office Address]`, and provides a **60 calendar day** grievance filing window.

PRIMARY RECORD SOURCE CITATIONS:
[Ex. 35]; [Emails with Burgard.txt](file:///home/annika/google_drive_intake/intake/Federal%20Complaint/Emails%20with%20Burgard.txt#L414-L440); `OC_ADA Grievance | Oregon City, OR.webarchive`; [ADA grievance website archive.txt](file:///home/annika/Documents/03_Federal_CivilRights_Action/ADA%20grievance%20website%20archive.txt).

DOCUMENTARY EVIDENCE (FULL VERBATIM TEXT):
[BURGARD & ERIKSSON DISCOVERY & WEBARCHIVE CAPTURE OF HASTILY ASSEMBLED DRAFT ADA WEBPAGE]
SOURCE: Webarchive Capture ('OC_ADA Grievance | Oregon City, OR.webarchive', Oct 26, 2025); Public Records Request Files (Ex. 35)
DATE OF CAPTURE: October 26, 2025 at 3:14 PM PST
DISCOVERED BY: Jacob Burgard & Annika Eriksson

OPERATIVE VERBATIM EXTRACT FROM CAPTURED DRAFT WEBPAGE:
URL: https://www.orcity.org/human-resources/page/americans-disabilities-act-title-ii-grievance-procedure
PAGE TITLE: Americans with Disabilities Act Title II Grievance Procedure | Oregon City, OR
STATUS AT CAPTURE: Unfinished draft published live to municipal website with placeholder template tokens:
- 'ADA Coordinator: [ADA Coordinator Name]'
- 'Email Contact: ada@oregoncity.gov'
- 'Telephone: (XXX) XXX-XXXX'
- 'Office Location: [ADA Coordinator Office Address], Oregon City, OR 97045'
- 'Filing Deadline: A grievance must be submitted in writing within sixty (60) calendar days of the alleged discriminatory act or practice.'

CHRONOLOGY OF MUNICIPAL TAKEDOWN & REPUBLICATION:
1. Sept 29, 2025: City FOIA officer discloses zero written ADA policies, procedures, or employee training records since Jan 1, 2020 in response to Jacob Burgard.
2. Oct 1, 2025: Burgard sends formal rebuttal citing 28 C.F.R. § 35.107, pointing out federal mandate for a published grievance procedure.
3. Oct 26, 2025: Burgard and Eriksson discover the City hastily posted the above unedited boilerplate template page to create the appearance of compliance. Captured into .webarchive format.
4. Oct 27–Nov 9, 2025: Webpage abruptly taken down by City IT / Human Resources (returned 404 / redirect).
5. Nov 10, 2025 at 2:57 AM: City re-publishes the ADA Grievance Procedure webpage, officially naming Human Resources Director Alexandra Troutman as ADA Coordinator, but SURREPTITIOUSLY HALVING THE GRIEVANCE FILING WINDOW FROM 60 CALENDAR DAYS DOWN TO 30 CALENDAR DAYS.

SOURCE: Oregon_City_Governance_Litigation_Source_Package.zip / supplement_sources
# Oregon City Public Voices and Oregon Homes Rulemaking
## Repository coverage, historical enforcement concerns, and an evidence-based assessment of Annika Eriksson's participation

Research date: October 13, 2026. This is targeted, preliminary public-records research—not an exhaustive audit, a finding of misconduct, or a legal opinion.

## 1. Findings in brief

**Other residents have raised concrete, related concerns over many years.** August 2013 minutes record residents challenging sidewalk/tree repair obligations, limited-income burdens, and deadlines. October 2015 minutes record calls for staff to reach the Commission without fear of retaliation and concerns about the City Attorney's relationship to the manager. November 2019 minutes document a neighbor dispute, a due-process concern, and questions about complaints against City staff. These are meaningful leads; the minutes establish that concerns were raised, not that the City violated the law. [C1–C3]

**Steve Varga remains unverified.** The recalled remark and his underlying situation were not located. Twenty recent recording-caption panels, three recently uploaded historical recordings, name variants, and relevant packet searches were checked. One additional historical-review recording could not be reliably opened. Automatic captions can miss names or wording; this search does not establish that he did not speak. No property, enforcement case, or motive has been assigned to him.

**Your written participation was formally recorded.** The published Phase One roster identifies Annika Eriksson as an individual bringing lived experience. Earlier submissions, the July 7 comment, and the September 28 submission updated September 30 are publicly preserved. The last of these is included in the October 22 Commission pack

RECORD 062 OF 107 | ITEM 5.6A | 5. PHASE V: FOIA DISCLOSURES, DOORSTEP TRO STANDOFF & CIRCUIT COURT HEARING (SEPTEMBER – DECEMBER 2025)
================================================================================
TRACK / LAYER: Forensic Record
DATE & TIME:   November 10, 2025 (2:57 AM)
TITLE:         City Takedown of Draft Page & Republication Cutting Grievance Deadline from 60 to 30 Days
MEDIUM:        Published City Webpage & Email Coordination (Email)
ACTORS:        From: City of Oregon City Web Administration / Alexandra Troutman -> To: Public / Annika Eriksson & Jacob Burgard [City Leadership]
SUBJECT:       `Republication of ADA Grievance Procedure with Shortened 30-Day Window`

FACTUAL & FORENSIC SUMMARY:
Following the exposure of its placeholder-filled draft page, Oregon City takes down the initial draft and quietly republishes the ADA Grievance portal (`https://www.orcity.org/3458/Americans-with-Disabilities-Act-ADA` and `/3459/ADA-Grievance`). The republished version replaces the template placeholders with Assistant City Manager Alexandra Troutman's official contact information and cites existing municipal documents (Transportation System Plan, Parks Master Plan, Sidewalk Program). Crucially, the City slashes the grievance filing deadline in half from **60 calendar days to 30 calendar days**, severely constricting citizen grievance rights. Annika Eriksson emails Jacob Burgard alerting him to the new 30-day limitation and requesting the original webarchive of the taken-down first draft, which Burgard transmits.

PRIMARY RECORD SOURCE CITATIONS:
[Ex. 35]; [Emails with Burgard.txt](file:///home/annika/google_drive_intake/intake/Federal%20Complaint/Emails%20with%20Burgard.txt#L425-L445); `https://www.orcity.org/3459/ADA-Grievance`.

TRACK / LAYER: Forensic Record
DATE & TIME:   November 15–16, 2025
TITLE:         Henderson Email Raising Plumbing Damage & Notice Objections
MEDIUM:        Email (Email)
ACTORS:        From: Ramon Henderson (`rh@hs-legal.com`) -> To: Annika Eriksson (`erikssona@icloud.com`) [Defense Counsel & CIS]
SUBJECT:       `RE: URGENT- SERVICE of Signed Temporary Restraining Order - Eriksson v. City of Oregon City`

FACTUAL & FORENSIC SUMMARY:
Henderson asserts the City cannot safely restore water under the TRO: *"Compliance with court's recent Order: As discussed today, the City cannot safely turn your water back on for the reasons stated in my prior email and explained to you and Mr. Buckhout this morning. Notably, turning the water back on could result in catastrophic failure to your home's plumbing system... damage to flooring, subflooring, drywall, electrical... mold... The City is not equipped to address the potential resulting damage, especially on short notice over the weekend."* He also objects to notice under ORCP 79 B.

================================================================================
TRACK / LAYER: Forensic Record
DATE & TIME:   Sunday, November 16, 2025 (Morning)
TITLE:         Henderson Doorstep Confrontation & Audio Recording
MEDIUM:        In-Person Recorded Doorstep Confrontation (In-Person / Inspection)
ACTORS:        From:  -> To:  [Other / General]
SUBJECT:       

FACTUAL & FORENSIC SUMMARY:
City utility crew arrives with Work Order 25-001106, removes the concrete barrier block, and connects the meter. However, retained counsel Henderson declares on recorded audio: *"Not happening today"* regarding opening the water valve unless Eriksson signs an un-drafted, unilateral release of liability waiving claims against the City. Buckhout and Eriksson refuse. After Henderson and the crew step away, Buckhout manually turns the curb stop valve himself, restoring water and terminating the 510-day physical shutoff.

RECORD 066 OF 107 | ITEM 5.10 | 5. PHASE V: FOIA DISCLOSURES, DOORSTEP TRO STANDOFF & CIRCUIT COURT HEARING (SEPTEMBER – DECEMBER 2025)
================================================================================
TRACK / LAYER: Forensic Record
DATE & TIME:   November 21, 2025
TITLE:         Preliminary Injunction Hearing (Judge Michael C. Wetzel)
MEDIUM:        In-Person Evidentiary Hearing (In-Person / Inspection)
ACTORS:        From:  -> To:  [Other / General]
SUBJECT:       

FACTUAL & FORENSIC SUMMARY:
Contested evidentiary hearing on whether to convert the TRO into a preliminary injunction. Henderson argues that plaintiffs tampered with the meter and that the account remains delinquent. Judge Wetzel dissolves the TRO and denies the preliminary injunction on procedural grounds, without reaching federal constitutional claims. Water remains on because the valve was already turned.

TRACK / LAYER: Forensic Record
DATE & TIME:   December 1, 2025 at 4:59 PM
TITLE:         Eriksson Constitutional Demand Letter
MEDIUM:        Email (Email)
ACTORS:        From: Annika Eriksson (`erikssona@icloud.com`) -> To: Ramon Henderson (`rh@hs-legal.com`) [Defense Counsel & CIS]
SUBJECT:       `Re: Interim Water Service Arrangement – 12054 Chapin Court`

FACTUAL & FORENSIC SUMMARY:
Detailed constitutional demand letter. Eriksson writes: *"This responds to your email of November 25, 2025, rejecting my good-faith offer to pay all current and ongoing water usage charges, plus a standard deposit... Demanding that I pay a contested debt in full to avoid termination of essential water service—when that debt relates entirely to disputed historical billing including unresolved charges for alleged tampering, and I stand ready to pay all current usage—constitutes a bad-faith abuse of governmental power... driven by my protected lawsuit activity. See Village of Willowbrook v. Olech; Nieves v. Bartlett... Final Demand: I hereby demand that the City of Oregon City immediately cease and desist from any threat or action to terminate water service... Should the City proceed with a shutoff, I will immediately seek a Temporary Restraining Order in federal court."* Demands written response by 5:00 PM Dec 2, 2025.

TRACK / LAYER: Forensic Record
DATE & TIME:   December 2, 2025
TITLE:         Henderson Rejection & Warning on Federal Action
MEDIUM:        Email (Email)
ACTORS:        From: Ramon Henderson (`rh@hs-legal.com`) -> To: Annika Eriksson (`erikssona@icloud.com`) [Defense Counsel & CIS]
SUBJECT:       `RE: Interim Water Service Arrangement – 12054 Chapin Court`

FACTUAL & FORENSIC SUMMARY:
Henderson responds: *"I continue to disagree with your baseless characterization that the City is acting retaliatory... Your water utility account is in arrears and has not been paid... The City's position and approach to you and your water utility account has been and continues to be neutral and complies with its standard policies and procedures... I believe any attempts to seek a TRO on the same grounds in federal court is wholly inappropriate. Additionally, I do not believe a federal court can even hear your request because it would be required to exercise Younger abstention due to the state proceedings. However, should you attempt to proceed with seeking a TRO in federal court, I again request proper and timely notice so that I may appear at any hearing. See FRCP 65."*

[CLACKAMAS COUNTY CIRCUIT COURT — CASE NO. 24CV21417 (JUDICIAL RULINGS OF JUDGE RODERICK A. BOUTIN)]
JUDICIAL OFFICER: Judge Roderick A. Boutin, Circuit Court Judge
PROCEDURAL RECORD:
- December 8, 2025 at 9:00 AM: Motion Hearing held before Judge Roderick A. Boutin on Plaintiff Annika Eriksson's ORCP 71 Motion for Relief from Order of Dismissal. Judge Boutin denied the motion as MOOT with respect to Defendant City of Oregon City, specifically finding that because the City had already been dismissed by Judge Weston in September 2025, no live justiciable controversy remained before him regarding municipal claims.
- January 12, 2026: Judge Roderick A. Boutin signs Limited Judgment of Dismissal (entered January 13, 2026), dismissing Plaintiff's claims against remaining defendants (Bank of New York Mellon, Shellpoint). MEDIUM:        Formal Letter (Mail & Email) (Email)

SUBJECT:       `Eriksson, Annika v. City of Oregon City, et al. / Clackamas Case No. 24CV21417`

FACTUAL & FORENSIC SUMMARY:
Henderson writes: *"As you are probably aware, the Court has now signed and entered the Limited Judgment of Dismissal with prejudice, which dismisses all of your claims against the City of Oregon City... During litigation, the City placed your water utility account in a suspended status. Following the Court's dismissal of your claims, the City views you as a standard utility customer again. As a standard utility customer, you are subject to the governance of the City code and policies. Please ensure that your water utility account is current and not in arrears or the City may disconnect your water service. Moving forward, please reach out to the appropriate City personnel for any further questions related to your water utility account."*

FACTUAL & FORENSIC SUMMARY:
Troutman asserts: *"A reasonable accommodation serves to provide access to City services and procedures for those with disabilities. For example, the City can provide aids at public meetings to help those with limited hearing. A reasonable accommodation will not mitigate or eliminate a customer's obligation to pay for utility service, whether it is a current or past due charge. Rather, as a standard utility customer, all due and past due expenditures must be paid in full, immediately. In the interest of encouraging you to come into compliance, the City is going to provide an additional two (2) weeks to pay all past due amounts, please remit payment by March 10th. The amount currently owed... is $4,138.55. If you believe you are entitled to an accommodation under the ADA, you must file your ADA request no later than March 3rd."*

RECORD 090 OF 107 | ITEM 7.1 | 7. PHASE VII: RENEWED VEGETATION CITATIONS, TROUTMAN CATEGORICAL DENIAL & $54,600 DEFAULT JUDGMENT (MAY – SEPTEMBER 2026)
================================================================================
TRACK / LAYER: Forensic Record
DATE & TIME:   May – June 2026
TITLE:         Jacob Wilson & Ryan Kersey Curtilage Citations, Work Truck Tagging & Towing Threats
MEDIUM:        Municipal Citations / Inspection Notices / Vehicle Tagging (Notice / Letter)
ACTORS:        From: Jacob Wilson (`jwilson@orcity.org`) & Ryan Kersey (Code Enforcement Division / Police Liaison) -> To: Annika Eriksson / Donald Buckhout / 12054 Chapin Court [Code Enforcement]
SUBJECT:       

FACTUAL & FORENSIC SUMMARY:
Officer Wilson and Officer Kersey initiate accelerated code enforcement citations and 24-hour warning notices regarding residential curtilage, demanding immediate removal of property maintenance materials, yard clippings, and dry vegetation. Concurrently, officers initiate targeted surveillance, tagging, and towing/impoundment threats against the household's turquoise 1995 Mazda B2300 pickup truck parked on the private driveway. In sworn declarations submitted in federal and state proceedings, Plaintiffs attest that this pickup truck is their sole operational vehicle for transporting domestic refuse/trash, hauling materials, and traveling to medical appointments, and that Donald Buckhout cannot drive due to a license suspension resulting from unresolved administrative fines incurred during prior displacement.

OREGON CITY MUNICIPAL COURT
                   TRIAL NOTICE - CODE ENFORCMENT, PARKING / IMPOUND

Citation # CE-014675-26
You are charged with the offense(s) of:
  CE-014675-26-1                    REMOVAL REQUIRED
  CE-014675-26-2                    PROHIBITED ACTIONS - solid waste
  CE-014675-26-3                    NUISANCES AFFECTING PEACE AND SAFETY

Your NOT GUILTY plea has been entered.
Trial will be to the Court in the Oregon City Municipal Court, 1234 Linn Ave., Oregon City, OR.
On 8/25/2026 at 3:00 PM.

                                                        READ CAREFULLY

When charged with a municipal violation, if you want counsel to represent you, you must hire an attorney. The City’s
attorney will not be present at trial unless you have counsel present. If you plan to have an attorney at trial, a letter of
representation must be submitted to the court prior to your scheduled court appearance, so the City’s attorney may be
present and your attorney may schedule a mutually agreed trial date. If you do not advise the Court, you will be
considered to have waived your right to counsel and the attorney will not be allowed to represent you.

     **No continuance or change of trial date will be allowed unless you hire an attorney OR granted by the Judge**

Failure to appear at trial, the court without further hearing, may enter a judgment against you for the bail amount and a
default judgment to code enforcement will be made. If you fail to appear you must contact Code Enforcement to avoid
extra fees and actions taken against you.

                                              **PROPER COURT ATTIRE REQUIRED**


I have read the above and understand it.
